        body: JSON.stringify({ text: transcript }),
      });

      const data: { questions?: ClassroomQuestion[]; error?: string } =
        await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || "فشل الاستنتاج.");
        return;
      }

      const newQ = data.questions ?? [];
      const merged = [...questions];
      const existing = new Set(
        questions.map((q) => q.inferredQuestion.toLowerCase()),
      );
      newQ.forEach((q) => {
        if (!existing.has(q.inferredQuestion.toLowerCase())) {
          merged.push(q);
        }
      });
      onQuestionsChange(merged);
    } catch {
      setError("تعذّر الاتصال بالخدمة.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card shadow-xs">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border/60 px-5 py-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary/10">
            <HelpCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">أسئلة القاعة</h2>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
              {questions.length > 0
                ? `${questions.length} سؤال مستنتج`
                : "استنتج الأسئلة من ردود الدكتور"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Button
          type="button"
          size="sm"
          onClick={handleScan}
          disabled={!canScan || loading}
          className="gap-2 rounded-xl bg-linear-to-l from-primary to-accent-1 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-md"
        >
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
              جارٍ الاستنتاج...
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <PlayCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              {questions.length > 0 ? "استنتج تاني" : "استنتج الأسئلة"}
            </>
          )}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="p-5">
        {/* Info banner */}
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-primary/20 bg-primary/5 p-3">
          <Info className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-primary" />
          <div className="text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
            <strong className="text-foreground">إزاي الميزة دي بتشتغل؟</strong>{" "}
            الطلاب البعيدين أسئلتهم مش بتتسمع في المايك. لكن الدكتور بيرد عليهم.
            الميزة دي بتاخد رد الدكتور، وتستنتج السؤال الأصلي تلقائياً.
          </div>
        </div>

        <AnimatePresence>
          {error && (
            <motion.p
              initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, height: "auto" }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
              className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-destructive"
            >
              {error}
            </motion.p>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>

        {questions.length === 0 && !loading && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border bg-muted/20 py-14 text-center">
            <MessageCircle className="h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground/40" />
            <p className="text-sm font-bold">في انتظار الاستنتاج</p>
            <p className="max-w-sm text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {canScan
                ? "اضغط (استنتج الأسئلة) عشان نلقط أسئلة القاعة من ردود الدكتور"
                : "سجّل الشرح الأول عشان نبدأ"}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {loading && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {[1, 2].map((i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-muted/40"
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {questions.length > 0 && !loading && (
          <ul className="space-y-4">
            {questions.map((q, i) => {
              const conf = CONFIDENCE_META[q.confidence];
              const ConfIcon = conf.icon;
              return (
                <motion.li
                  key={q.id}
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
                  className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-muted/20"
                >
                  {/* Question row */}
                  <div className="border-b border-border/60 bg-primary/5 p-4">
                    <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-primary">
                        ❓ سؤال محتمل من القاعة
                      </span>
                      <span
                        className={cn(
                          "flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold",
                          conf.bg,
                          conf.color,
                        )}
                      >
                        <ConfIcon className="h-2.5 w-2.5" />
                        {conf.label}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
                      {q.inferredQuestion}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Answer row */}
                  <div className="flex gap-3 p-4">
                    <ArrowLeft className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <span className="mb-1 block text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                        ✅ إجابة الدكتور
                      </span>
                      <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
                        {q.answer}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </motion.li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

========== END ==========

========== components\ClozeOverlay.tsx ==========
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "motion/react";
import { Brain, Check, X, RotateCcw, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface ClozeCell {
  id: string;
  value: string;
}

interface ClozeOverlayProps {
  cells: ClozeCell[];
  onClose: () => void;
  children: (renderCell: (cell: ClozeCell) => React.ReactNode) => React.ReactNode;
}

/**
 * Cloze mode — hides a subset of cells, shows shuffled chips below,
 * user clicks chip then clicks slot to place it. Immediate feedback.
 */
export default function ClozeOverlay({ cells, onClose, children }: ClozeOverlayProps) {
  const [hiddenIds, setHiddenIds] = React.useState<Set<string>>(() => {
    // hide 60% of cells randomly
    const shuffled = [...cells].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    const count = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(cells.length * 0.6));
    return new Set(shuffled.slice(0, count).map((c) => c.id));
  });

  const [placed, setPlaced] = React.useState<Record<string, string | null>>({});
  const [selectedChip, setSelectedChip] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [results, setResults] = React.useState<Record<string, "correct" | "wrong">>({});

  const usedChips = new Set(
    Object.values(placed).filter((v): v is string => !!v)
  );

  const availableChips = React.useMemo(() => {
    const hidden = cells.filter((c) => hiddenIds.has(c.id));
    return hidden
      .filter((c) => !usedChips.has(c.value))
      .sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [cells, hiddenIds, Object.values(placed).join("|")]);

  const handleChipClick = (value: string) => {
    setSelectedChip(selectedChip === value ? null : value);
  };

  const handleCellClick = (cellId: string) => {
    if (!selectedChip) return;
    const target = cells.find((c) => c.id === cellId);
    if (!target) return;

    const isCorrect = target.value === selectedChip;
    setPlaced((p) => ({ ...p, [cellId]: selectedChip }));
    setResults((r) => ({ ...r, [cellId]: isCorrect ? "correct" : "wrong" }));
    setSelectedChip(null);
  };

  const renderCell = (cell: ClozeCell): React.ReactNode => {
    if (!hiddenIds.has(cell.id)) {
      return <span className="text-foreground">{cell.value}</span>;
    }
    const placedValue = placed[cell.id];
    const result = results[cell.id];

    if (placedValue && result === "correct") {
      return (
        <motion.span
          initial={{ scale: 0.9 }}
          animate={{ scale: 1 }}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-500/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-bold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400"
        >
          <Check className="h-3 w-3" />
          {placedValue}
        </motion.span>
      );
    }
    if (placedValue && result === "wrong") {
      return (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
          <motion.span
            initial={{ x: 0 }}
            animate={{ x: [0, -3, 3, -3, 0] }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
            className="rounded-md bg-rose-500/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-rose-700 line-through dark:text-rose-400"
          >
            <X className="inline h-3 w-3" /> {placedValue}
          </motion.span>
          <span className="rounded-md bg-emerald-500/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">
            ← {cell.value}
          </span>
        </span>
      );
    }

    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => handleCellClick(cell.id)}
        disabled={!selectedChip}
        className={cn(
          "inline-flex min-w-[60px] items-center justify-center rounded-md border-2 border-dashed px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold transition-all",
          selectedChip
            ? "border-primary bg-primary/10 text-primary cursor-pointer hover:bg-primary/20"
            : "border-muted-foreground/40 bg-muted/30 text-muted-foreground"
        )}
      >
        ___
      </button>
    );
  };

  const total = hiddenIds.size;
  const correctCount = Object.values(results).filter((r) => r === "correct").length;
  const done = correctCount + Object.values(results).filter((r) => r === "wrong").length;
  const percent = total > 0 ? Math.round((correctCount / total) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
      className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-primary/30 bg-primary/5 p-4"
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary/15">
            <Brain className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">
              اختبر نفسك — اكمل الفراغات
            </p>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
              {done}/{total} · صح: {correctCount} · {percent}%
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button
            type="button"
            size="xs"
            variant="outline"
            onClick={() => {
              setPlaced({});
              setResults({});
              setSelectedChip(null);
            }}
            className="gap-1"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-3 w-3" />
            من جديد
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="button"
            size="xs"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="gap-1 bg-linear-to-l from-primary to-accent-1"
          >
            <EyeOff className="h-3 w-3" />
            إخفاء
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Content with cloze cells */}
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-background p-4">
        {children(renderCell)}
      </div>

      {/* Chips tray */}
      <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-primary/20 bg-background p-3">
        <p className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-primary">
          <Eye className="h-3 w-3" />
          اضغط على الكلمة ثم اضغط على الفراغ
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {availableChips.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              ✓ خلصت كل الكلمات
            </p>
          ) : (
            availableChips.map((chip, i) => (
              <motion.button
                key={`${chip.value}-${i}`}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleChipClick(chip.value)}
                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                transition={{ delay: i * 0.04 }}
                className={cn(
                  "rounded-lg border-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all",
                  selectedChip === chip.value
                    ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground scale-105"
                    : "border-border bg-muted/40 text-foreground hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-muted"
                )}
              >
                {chip.value}
              </motion.button>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}
========== END ==========

========== components\ComparisonTablePanel.tsx ==========
"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "motion/react";
import {
  Table as TableIcon,
  Loader2,
  PlayCircle,
  Trash2,
  Sparkles,
  Quote,
  Info,
  Brain,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ErrorState } from "@/components/ui/ErrorState";
import ClozeOverlay from "@/components/ClozeOverlay";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { ComparisonTable } from "@/lib/ai-types";

interface ComparisonTablePanelProps {
  transcript: string;
}

export default function ComparisonTablePanel({
  transcript,
}: ComparisonTablePanelProps) {
  const [tables, setTables] = React.useState<ComparisonTable[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  const [clozeMode, setClozeMode] = React.useState(false);

  const canGenerate = transcript.trim().length >= 50 && !loading;

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    if (!canGenerate) return;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/visualize-comparison", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ text: transcript }),
      });
      const data: { comparisons?: ComparisonTable[]; error?: string } =
        await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(data.error || "فشل استخراج المقارنات.");
        return;
      }
      setTables(data.comparisons ?? []);
      setClozeMode(false);
    } catch {
      setError("تعذّر الاتصال بالخدمة.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card shadow-xs">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border/60 px-5 py-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-linear-to-br from-primary to-accent-1">
            <TableIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold">جداول المقارنة</h2>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
              {tables.length > 0
                ? `${tables.length} مقارنة`
                : "استخرج المقارنات من كلام الدكتور"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {tables.length > 0 && !clozeMode && (
            <Button
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={() => setClozeMode(true)}
              className="gap-2 border-violet-500/40 text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400"
            >
              <Brain className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              اختبر نفسك
            </Button>
          )}

          {!clozeMode && tables.length > 0 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setTables([])}
              aria-label="حذف"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 text-destructive transition-colors hover:bg-destructive/15"
            >
              <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}

          {!clozeMode && (
            <Button
              type="button"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleGenerate}
              disabled={!canGenerate}
              className="gap-2 bg-linear-to-l from-primary to-accent-1 text-xs font-bold"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                  جارٍ الاستخراج...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <PlayCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  {tables.length > 0 ? "استخرج تاني" : "استخرج المقارنات"}
                </>
              )}
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="p-5">
        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          {error && (
            <motion.div
              key="error"
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8 }}
              className="mb-4"
            >
              <ErrorState
                category="provider"
                title="مش قادرين نستخرج المقارنات"
                description={error}
                hint="تأكد من المحاضرة فيها مقارنة صريحة"
                onRetry={() => {
                  setError(null);
                  handleGenerate();
                }}
              />
            </motion.div>
          )}

          {clozeMode ? (
            <motion.div
              key="cloze"
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            >
              <ComparisonCloze tables={tables} onExit={() => setClozeMode(false)} />
            </motion.div>
          ) : (
            <motion.div
              key="view"
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
            >
              {/* Legend */}
              {tables.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-xl border border-border bg-muted/20 px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
                    <span className="font-bold text-foreground">من المحاضرة</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500" />
                    <span className="font-bold text-foreground">مستنتج</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {tables.length === 0 && !loading && !error && (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border bg-muted/20 py-14 text-center">
                  <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground/40" />
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">
                    {canGenerate
                      ? "اضغط (استخرج المقارنات)"
                      : "سجّل 50 حرف على الأقل الأول"}
                  </p>
                  <p className="max-w-md text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {canGenerate
                      ? "هنلقط كل المقارنات: let vs var، TCP vs UDP، SQL vs NoSQL..."
                      : "المقارنات بتظهر لما الدكتور يقارن بين حاجتين"}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {loading && (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 py-14">
                  <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-primary" />
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    بندوّر على مقارنات في المحاضرة...
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {tables.length > 0 && !loading && (
                <div className="space-y-6">
                  {tables.map((table, idx) => (
                    <ComparisonCard key={table.id} table={table} index={idx} />
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Cloze wrapper for comparison tables                                */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

function ComparisonCloze({
  tables,
  onExit,
}: {
  tables: ComparisonTable[];
  onExit: () => void;
}) {
  const [activeTableIdx, setActiveTableIdx] = React.useState(0);
  const table = tables[activeTableIdx];
  if (!table) return null;

  // اجمع كل خلايا القيم كـ cloze cells
  const allCells = React.useMemo(() => {
    const cells: Array<{ id: string; value: string; criterion: string; item: string }> = [];
    table.criteria.forEach((criterion, ci) => {
      criterion.values.forEach((cell, vi) => {
        if (cell.value && cell.value !== "—") {
          cells.push({
            id: `c${ci}-v${vi}`,
            value: cell.value,
            criterion: criterion.name,
            item: table.items[vi]?.name ?? "",
          });
        }
      });
    });
    return cells;
  }, [table]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {tables.length > 1 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {tables.map((t, i) => (
            <button
              key={t.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveTableIdx(i)}
              className={cn(
                "rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition-colors",
                i === activeTableIdx
                  ? "border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 text-primary"
                  : "border-border bg-background text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted"
              )}
            >
              {i + 1}. {t.title}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <ClozeOverlay cells={allCells} onClose={onExit}>
        {(renderCell) => (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <div className="mb-3">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{table.title}</h3>
              {table.subtitle && (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{table.subtitle}</p>
              )}
            </div>
            <table className="w-full text-right text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-border">
                  <th className="bg-muted/40 p-3 font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                    المعيار
                  </th>
                  {table.items.map((item, i) => (
                    <th
                      key={i}
                      className="p-3 font-bold text-foreground"
                    >
                      {item.name}
                    </th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {table.criteria.map((criterion, ci) => (
                  <tr key={ci} className="border-b border-border last:border-0">
                    <td className="bg-muted/20 p-3 align-top text-[11px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                      {criterion.name}
                    </td>
                    {criterion.values.map((cell, vi) => {
                      const cellId = `c${ci}-v${vi}`;
                      const isHidden = cell.value && cell.value !== "—";
                      return (
                        <td key={vi} className="p-3 align-top">
                          {isHidden ? (
                            renderCell({ id: cellId, value: cell.value })
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-muted-foreground">—</span>
                          )}
                        </td>
                      );
                    })}
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </ClozeOverlay>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

function ComparisonCard({
  table,
  index,
}: {
  table: ComparisonTable;
  index: number;
}) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ delay: index * 0.06 }}
      className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-background"
    >
      <div className="border-b border-border bg-linear-to-l from-primary/8 to-accent-2/5 px-5 py-3.5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/15 text-[10px] font-bold text-primary">
            {index + 1}
          </span>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-foreground">{table.title}</h3>
        </div>
        {table.subtitle && (
          <p className="mt-1 pr-8 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            {table.subtitle}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto p-4">
        <table className="w-full text-right text-xs">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b border-border">
              <th className="bg-muted/40 p-3 font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                المعيار
              </th>
              {table.items.map((item, i) => (
                <th
                  key={i}
                  className={cn(
                    "p-3 font-bold",
                    item.source === "lecture"
                      ? "text-foreground"
                      : "text-muted-foreground"
                  )}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    {item.source === "inferred" && (
                      <span
                        className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-500"
                        title="مستنتج"
                      />
                    )}
                    {item.name}
                  </div>
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {table.criteria.map((criterion, ci) => (
              <tr
                key={ci}
                className="border-b border-border last:border-0 hover:bg-muted/20"
              >
                <td className="bg-muted/20 p-3 align-top text-[11px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                  {criterion.name}
                </td>
                {criterion.values.map((cell, vi) => (
                  <td key={vi} className="p-3 align-top">
                    <div className="space-y-1">
                      <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5">
                        <span
                          className={cn(
                            "mt-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full",
                            cell.source === "lecture"
                              ? "bg-emerald-500"
                              : "bg-amber-500"
                          )}
                        />
                        <span
                          className={cn(
                            "leading-relaxed",
                            cell.source === "lecture"
                              ? "text-foreground"
                              : "text-muted-foreground italic"
                          )}
                        >
                          {cell.value}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {cell.quote && (
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-1 rounded-md bg-primary/5 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] leading-snug text-primary/90">
                          <Quote className="mt-0.5 h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0" />
                          <span className="italic">
                            &ldquo;{cell.quote}&rdquo;
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                ))}
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-border bg-muted/20 px-4 py-2 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
        <Info className="h-3 w-3" />
        <span>أخضر = من المحاضرة · أصفر = مستنتج من معرفة عامة</span>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}
